SELECT [r].[RecipeID], [r].[MaterialID], [r].[VersionNumber], [r].[BatchSize], [r].[UomId], [r].[Status], [r].[ApprovedBy], [r].[ApprovedDate], [r].[CreatedAt], [r].[EffectiveDate], [r].[RecipeName], [r].[Note], CASE
    WHEN [m].[MaterialID] IS NULL THEN CAST(1 AS bit)
    ELSE CAST(0 AS bit)
END, [m].[MaterialID], [m].[MaterialName], [m].[MaterialCode], CASE
    WHEN [u].[UomID] IS NULL THEN CAST(1 AS bit)
    ELSE CAST(0 AS bit)
END, [u].[UomName]
